oscillator = audioContext.createOscillator(); harmonic = audioContext.createOscillator(); engineGain = audioContext.createGain(); ambienceGain = audioContext.createGain();\n oscillator.type = 'sawtooth'; ambienceGain?.gain.setTargetAtTime(0, audioContext.currentTime, 0.08); }\n }\n function horn() {\n population.horn(car.position.x, car.position.z);\n if (!sound || !audioContext) return;\n const gain = audioContext.createGain(); 'Headlights on.' : 'Headlights off.'); emit(); }\n function setTime(value: 'golden' | 'day' | 'night') {\n preset = value; const night = value === 'night';\n skyMaterial.uniforms.top.value.set(night ? 'R' : Math.abs(speed) < 0.5 ? 'N' : String(Math.min(6, Math.max(1, Math.ceil(Math.abs(speed) * 3.6 / 36))));\n let routeDistance = 0; const plottedRoute = route.length ? [{ x: car.position.x, z: car.position.z }, ...route.slice(1)] : [];\n plottedRoute.forEach((point, i) => { if (i) routeDistance += Math.hypot(point.x - plottedRoute[i - 1].x, point.z - plottedRoute[i - 1].z); });\n onUpdate({ speed: Math.round(Math.hypot(vx, vz) * 3.6), gear, boost, boosting, distance, position: { x: car.position.x, z: car.position.z }, heading, district: assets.districtAt(car.position.x, car.position.z), camera: cameraNames[cameraMode], paused, landmarks: landmarks.map(l => ({ ...l })), waypoint: waypoint ? { ...waypoint } : null, route: plottedRoute, routeDistance, map: mapData, traffic: population.traffic.filter(v => v.active).map(v => ({ x: v.x, z: v.z })), rpm: Math.round(rpm), headlights: headlightsOn, weather, safetyBrake });\n }\n function reset() {\n car.position.set(assets.spawn.x, 0.025, assets.spawn.z); heading = assets.spawn.heading; speed = vx = vz = steering = 0; boost = 100; moved = false; shake = 0; keys.clear();\n cameraMode = 0; chassis.visible = true; model.exterior.visible = true; model.interior.visible = true;\n camera.position.set(car.position.x + 4, 4.3, car.position.z + 9.3); smoothLook.set(car.position.x, 1, car.position.z - 7); routePlan(); emit();\n }\n function cycleCamera() {\n cameraMode = (cameraMode + 1) % 4; chassis.visible = cameraMode !== 1; model.exterior.visible = cameraMode !== 2; model.interior.visible = cameraMode !== 1; emit();\n }\n const resize = () => {\n const width = Math.max(1, mount.clientWidth), height = Math.max(1, mount.clientHeight), ratio = renderer.getPixelRatio();\n camera.aspect = width / height; camera.updateProjectionMatrix(); renderer.setSize(width, height);\n baseTarget.setSize(Math.round(width * ratio), Math.round(height * ratio)); bloomTargetA.setSize(Math.max(1, Math.round(width * ratio / 3)), Math.max(1, Math.round(height * ratio / 3))); bloomTargetB.setSize(bloomTargetA.width, bloomTargetA.height);\n };\n const observer = new ResizeObserver(resize); observer.observe(mount); resize();\n function keyDown(event: KeyboardEvent) {\n const key = event.key.toLowerCase();\n if (key === 'escape' && !event.repeat) { event.preventDefault(); onAction('pause'); return; }\n if (event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey || event.altKey) return;\n const target = event.target instanceof Element ? event.target : document.body;\n if (['INPUT', 'SELECT', 'TEXTAREA'].includes(target.tagName)) return;\n if (target.closest('button') && key === ' ') return;\n if (['arrowup', 'arrowdown', 'arrowleft', 'arrowright', ' '].includes(key)) event.preventDefault();\n if (!paused && ['w', 'a', 's', 'd', 'arrowup', 'arrowdown', 'arrowleft', 'arrowright'].includes(key)) renderer.domElement.focus({ preventScroll: true });\n if (!event.repeat) {\n if (key === 'c' && !paused) { cycleCamera(); onAction('camera'); }\n if (key === 'r' && !paused) { reset(); onAction('reset'); }\n if (key === 'h' && !paused) horn();\n if (key === 'e' && !paused) toggleHeadlights();\n if (key === 'm') onAction('map'); if (key === 'f') onAction('fullscreen');\n }\n keys.add(key);\n }\n const keyUp = (event: KeyboardEvent) => keys.delete(event.key.toLowerCase());\n const clearKeys = () => keys.clear();\n window.addEventListener('keydown', keyDown); window.addEventListener('keyup', keyUp); window.addEventListener('blur', clearKeys);\n const visibility = () => { if (document.hidden) clearKeys(); }; document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', visibility);\n setTime('night'); setWeather('rain');\n population.update(0, 0, { x: car.position.x, z: car.position.z });\n let previousTime = performance.now();\n renderer.setAnimationLoop((now: number) => {\n if (disposed) return;\n const dt = Math.min(Math.max((now - previousTime) / 1000, 0.001), 0.05); previousTime = now;\n if (!paused) {\n time += dt;\n const forward = keys.has('w') || keys.has('arrowup'), reverse = keys.has('s') || keys.has('arrowdown');\n const left = keys.has('a') || keys.has('arrowleft'), right = keys.has('d') || keys.has('arrowright'), handbrake = keys.has(' ');\n safetyBrake = speed > 0 && population.pedestrianAhead(car.position.x, car.position.z, heading, Math.max(6.4, speed * speed / 50 + 2.4));\n boosting = keys.has('shift') && forward && !handbrake && boost > 1 && !safetyBrake;\n const acceleration = safetyBrake ? -Math.min(speed / dt, 25) : forward ? speed < -0.8 ? 21 : boosting ? 17 : 9.8 : reverse ? speed > 0.8 ? -23 : -5.0 : 0;\n const drag = speed * Math.abs(speed) * 0.0034 + Math.sign(speed) * (forward || reverse ? 0.15 : 0.8);\n speed += (acceleration - drag) * dt;\n speed *= Math.exp(-dt * (handbrake ? 1.3 : forward || reverse ? 0.055 : 0.25));\n speed = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(speed, -10, boosting ? 67 : 53);\n if (Math.abs(speed) < 0.06) speed = 0;\n steering = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(steering, (left ? 1 : 0) - (right ? 1 : 0), 1 - Math.exp(-dt * 5.5));\n const steerAngle = steering * 0.53 / (1 + Math.abs(speed) * 0.043);\n const yawRate = speed / 2.8 * Math.tan(steerAngle) * (handbrake ? 1.5 : 1);\n heading += yawRate * dt;\n const grip = handbrake ? 1.0 : weather === 'rain' ? 6.1 : 9.5;\n vx = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(vx, -Math.sin(heading) * speed, 1 - Math.exp(-dt * grip)); vz = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(vz, -Math.cos(heading) * speed, 1 - Math.exp(-dt * grip));\n const oldX = car.position.x, oldZ = car.position.z;\n car.position.x += vx * dt; car.position.z += vz * dt;\n const pos = car.position;\n let collision = pos.x < -545 || pos.x > 545 || pos.z < -545 || pos.z > 545 || pos.x > 324 && pos.x < 376 && Math.abs(pos.z - Math.round(pos.z / 100) * 100) > 9.3;\n if (!collision) for (const obstacle of assets.colliders) {\n if (Math.abs(pos.x - obstacle.x) > obstacle.w / 2 + 1.1 || Math.abs(pos.z - obstacle.z) > obstacle.d / 2 + 1.1) continue;\n const nx = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(pos.x, obstacle.x - obstacle.w / 2, obstacle.x + obstacle.w / 2), nz = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(pos.z, obstacle.z - obstacle.d / 2, obstacle.z + obstacle.d / 2);\n if (Math.hypot(pos.x - nx, pos.z - nz) < 1.05) { collision = true; break; }\n }\n if (!collision) for (const vehicle of population.traffic) {\n if (!vehicle.active || Math.hypot(pos.x - vehicle.x, pos.z - vehicle.z) > 7) continue;\n const dx = pos.x - vehicle.x, dz = pos.z - vehicle.z, lateral = dx * Math.cos(vehicle.heading) - dz * Math.sin(vehicle.heading), longitudinal = dx * Math.sin(vehicle.heading) + dz * Math.cos(vehicle.heading);\n if (Math.abs(lateral) < 1.68 && Math.abs(longitudinal) < vehicle.model.length * 0.5 + 1.3) { collision = true; break; }\n }\n if (collision) {\n pos.x = oldX; pos.z = oldZ; shake = Math.min(Math.abs(speed) / 70, 0.25); speed *= -0.18; vx *= -0.12; vz *= -0.12;\n if (time - lastNotice > 5) { lastNotice = time; onAction('notice', 'Easy does it. R puts you back on the road.'); }\n }\n const actualSpeed = Math.hypot(vx, vz); distance += actualSpeed * dt; if (actualSpeed > 1) moved = true;\n boost = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(boost + (boosting ? -21 : 8.5) * dt, 0, 100);\n car.rotation.y = heading;\n const transfer = Math.min(Math.abs(speed) * 0.0022, 0.065) * steering;\n chassis.rotation.z = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(chassis.rotation.z, -transfer, 1 - Math.exp(-dt * 6));\n chassis.rotation.x = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(chassis.rotation.x, (reverse || safetyBrake) && speed > 1 ? -0.035 : forward ? 0.016 : 0, 1 - Math.exp(-dt * 5));\n const nx = Math.abs(pos.x - Math.round(pos.x / 100) * 100), nz = Math.abs(pos.z - Math.round(pos.z / 100) * 100);\n const curb = Math.min(nx, nz) > 10 && Math.min(nx, nz) < 14 || pos.x > 324 && pos.x < 376;\n pos.y = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(pos.y, (curb ? function mergeGroup(root: Three.Group) {\n root.updateMatrixWorld(true);\n const inverse = root.matrixWorld.clone().invert();\n const grouped = new Map();\n root.traverse(object => {\n if (!(object instanceof THREE.Mesh)) return;\n const material = object.material as Three.Material;\n if (!grouped.has(material.uuid)) grouped.set(material.uuid, { material, positions: [], normals: [], uvs: [], indices: [] });\n const group = grouped.get(material.uuid)!, geometry = object.geometry, positions = geometry.getAttribute('position'), normals = geometry.getAttribute('normal'), uv = geometry.getAttribute('uv');\n const matrix = inverse.clone().multiply(object.matrixWorld), normalMatrix = new THREE.Matrix3().getNormalMatrix(matrix), vector = new THREE.Vector3(), offset = group.positions.length / 3;\n for (let i = 0; i < positions.count; i++) {\n vector.fromBufferAttribute(positions, i).applyMatrix4(matrix); group.positions.push(vector.x, vector.y, vector.z);\n if (normals) vector.fromBufferAttribute(normals, i).applyNormalMatrix(normalMatrix); else vector.set(0, 1, 0);\n group.normals.push(vector.x, vector.y, vector.z); group.uvs.push(uv ? uv.getX(i) : 0, uv ? uv.getY(i) : 0);\n }\n const index = geometry.getIndex(); if (index) for (let i = 0; i < index.count; i++) group.indices.push(offset + index.getX(i)); else for (let i = 0; i < positions.count; i++) group.indices.push(offset + i);\n });\n root.clear();\n for (const group of grouped.values()) {\n const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); geometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(group.positions, 3)); geometry.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(group.normals, 3)); geometry.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(group.uvs, 2)); geometry.setIndex(group.indices); geometry.computeBoundingSphere(); geometries.add(geometry);\n part(root, geometry, group.material, 0, 0, 0);\n }\n }\n mergeGroup(staticRoot);\n wheels.forEach(({ wheel }) => mergeGroup(wheel));\n if (!player) mergeGroup(interior);\n chassis.scale.z = lengthScale;\n const exterior = staticRoot;\n return { car, chassis, exterior, interior, bodyMaterial, glass, wheels, brakeMaterial, headMaterial, amberMaterial, steeringWheel, headlights, flames, length: 4.8 * lengthScale, radius: bus ? 2.1 : 1.3, variant };\n}PK\nzqL]\nsrc/utils/PK\nzqL]8src/utils/cn.tsimport { clsx, type ClassValue } from \"clsx\";\nimport { twMerge } from \"tailwind-merge\";\n\nexport function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]) {\n
